\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-H\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right],

where we use the fact

\displaystyle\left|\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right|(s,\bm{a})\leq

\displaystyle\min\left\{H,\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}\left\|\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right\|_{\infty}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle H\min\left\{1,\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\min\left\{1,f^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right\}

and the last row uses the induction assumption.

Therefore, we have proved equation
9
. We then apply
h=1
to equation
9
, and get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-H\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right].

Next we are going to bound the second term, let
g_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\min\left\{f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}
and apply Lemma
B.3
to
g_{h}
, we have for
h=1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[\min\left\{f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]\leq\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho_{1}^{(n)}}\left[\left(f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]}\leq\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}.

And
\forall h\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\min\left\{f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]+d\lambda+n\zeta^{(n)}},1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda+n\zeta^{(n)}},1\right\}\right].

Note that we here use the fact
\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\leq 1,\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
and
\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
. Then according to our choice of
\alpha^{(n)}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}},1\right\}\right].

Combining all things together,

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\}\right]-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},

which proves the inequality.
∎

Lemma B.7

(Pessimism)
.

Consider an episode
n\in[N]
and set
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\sqrt{nA\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\right)
. When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds, we have

\displaystyle\underline{v}_{i}^{(n)}(s)-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}(s)\leq H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},\quad\forall n\in[N],i\in[M].

Proof.

Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}
, then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi_{h}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h}.

A direct conclusion of the event
\mathcal{E}
is we can find an absolute constant
c
, such that

\displaystyle\beta_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}},H\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H].

Again, we prove the following inequality by induction:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s)\right]\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+H\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right],\quad\forall h\in[H].

(10)

•

When
h=H
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{V}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{H,i}(s)\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{Q}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Q^{\pi^{(n)}}_{H,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+H\min\left\{f_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]

•

Suppose the statement is true for
h+1
, then for step
h
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Q^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)(s,\bm{a})-\left(P^{\star}_{h}V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\left(\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+H\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+H\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right].

where we use the fact

\displaystyle\left|\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right|(s,\bm{a})\leq

\displaystyle\min\left\{H,\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}\left\|V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right\|_{\infty}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle H\min\left\{1,\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle H\min\left\{1,f^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right\}

and the last row uses the induction assumption.

The remaining steps are exactly the same as the proof in Lemma
B.5
or Lemma
B.6
, we may prove

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[\min\left\{f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]\leq\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}},1\right\}\right],\quad\forall h\geq 2.

Combining all things together, we get

\displaystyle\underline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\underline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+H\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\}\right]+H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},

which has finished the proof.
∎

Lemma B.8
.

For the model-based algorithm, when we pick
\lambda=\Theta\left(d\log\frac{NH|\Phi|}{\delta}\right)
,
\zeta^{(n)}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{n}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HN}{\delta}\right)
and
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\sqrt{nA\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\right)
, with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim H^{3}d^{2}AN^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HN}{\delta}.

Proof.

With our choice of
\lambda
and
\zeta^{(n)}
, according to Lemma
B.2
, we know
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability
1-\delta
. Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\sqrt{A\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HN}{\delta}+d^{2}\log\frac{NH|\Phi|}{\delta}}\right)=O\left(dH\sqrt{A\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HN}{\delta}}\right)

Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}
. According to the definition of the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi_{h}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h}.

(11)

By definition, we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n)}=\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}\right\}+2H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}.

For each fixed
i\in[M],h\in[H]
and
n\in[N]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi^{(n)}_{h}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)-\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi^{(n)}_{h}}\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[2\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[2\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h+1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[2\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})+2H^{2}f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h+1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)\right].

Note that we use the fact
\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)
is upper bounded by
2H^{2}
, which can be proved easily using induction using the fact that
\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\leq H
. Applying the above formula recursively to
\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h+1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]
, one gets the following result (or more formally, one can prove by induction, just like what we did in Lemma
B.5
, Lemma
B.6
and Lemma
B.7
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)\right]\leq 2\underbrace{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]}_{(a)}+2H^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]}_{(b)}.

(12)

First, we calculate the first term (a) in Inequality equation
12
. Following Lemma
B.4
and noting the bonus
\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}
is
O(H)
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}},H\right\}\right]

(From equation
11
)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{{nA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}}\right]+H^{2}d\lambda}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sqrt{A\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[\left\|\hat{\phi}_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{1},\hat{\phi}_{1}^{(n)}}}\right]}.

Note that we use the fact that
B=H
when applying Lemma
B.4
. In addition, we have

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left\|\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\textrm{Tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]\left(n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d.

Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sqrt{{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}/n}.

Second, we calculate the term (b) in inequality equation
12
. Following Lemma
B.4
and noting that
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}
is upper-bounded by
2
(i.e.,
B=2
in Lemma
B.4
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{nA\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]+d\lambda}+\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{1}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{nA\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi_{n}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},

where in the second inequality, we use
\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho_{h}^{(n)}}\left[\left(f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
, and in the last line, recall
\sqrt{nA\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\lesssim\alpha^{(n)}/H
. Then, by combining the above calculation of the term (a) and term (b) in inequality equation
12
, we have:

\displaystyle\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}\right).

Taking maximum over
i
on both sides and using the definition of
\Delta^{(n)}
, we get

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n)}=

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}\right\}+2H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}\right).

Hereafter, we take the dominating term out. Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\leq\sqrt{N\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right]}

(CS inequality)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{N\left(\log\det\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}[\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}]\right)-\log\det(\lambda I_{d})\right)}

(Lemma
E.2
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{dN\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}.

(Potential function bound, Lemma
E.3
noting
\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(s,\bm{a})\|_{2}\leq 1
for any
(s,\bm{a})
.)

Finally,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim

\displaystyle H\left(\sqrt{dN\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d}\right)}\sqrt{{dA\left(\alpha^{(N)}\right)^{2}}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sqrt{\frac{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{3}\left(\frac{1}{H}\sqrt{dN\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle H^{2}d\sqrt{NA\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}

(Some algebra. We take the dominating term out. Note that
\alpha^{(n)}
is increasing in
n
)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle H^{3}d^{2}AN^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HN}{\delta}.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
4.1

Proof.

For any fixed episode
n
and agent
i
, by Lemma
B.5
, Lemma
B.6
and Lemma
B.7
, we have

\displaystyle v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right)\leq\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}+2H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}\leq\Delta^{(n)}.

Taking maximum over
i
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right\}\right)\leq\Delta^{(n)}.

(13)

From Lemma
B.8
, with probability
1-\delta
, we can ensure

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim H^{3}d^{2}AN^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HN}{\delta}.

Therefore, according to Lemma
E.4
, when we pick
N
to be

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{H^{6}d^{4}A^{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{HdA|\mathcal{M}|}{\delta\varepsilon}\right)\right),

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\leq\varepsilon.

On the other hand, from equation
13
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\hat{\pi}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\hat{\pi}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\hat{\pi}}_{i}-v^{\hat{\pi}}_{i}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n^{\star})}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n^{\star})}=\min_{n\in[N]}\Delta^{(n)}\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\leq\varepsilon,

which has finished the proof.
∎

Appendix C
Analysis of the Model-Free Method

For the model-free method, throughout this section we assume the Markov game is a block Markov game.

C.1
Non-Parametric Transition Model

Define

\displaystyle\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\bm{a})=\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\left(\sum_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\in\mathcal{D}}\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}+\lambda I_{d}\right)^{-1}\sum_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}},\tilde{s}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{D}}\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\bm{1}_{\tilde{s}^{\prime}=s^{\prime}},

where
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{D}^{(n)}_{h}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{D}}_{h}^{(n)}
. The formulation of
\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}
makes the transition operator
\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}f
be exactly equal to the least square estimator, i.e.,

\displaystyle\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}f\right)(s,\bm{a})=\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\left(\sum_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\in\mathcal{D}}\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}+\lambda I_{d}\right)^{-1}\sum_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}},\tilde{s}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{D}}\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})f(s^{\prime}).

Furthermore, when
\phi^{(n)}_{h}
belongs to the set of one-hot vectors, one can verify that we always have
\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\bm{a})\geq 0
and
\sum_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\bm{a})\leq 1
.

C.2
Construction of
\mathcal{N}_{h}
and
\mathcal{F}_{h}

Let
\mathcal{C}_{h}=\{\Sigma_{h}:\Sigma_{h}=\lambda I_{d}+\sum_{k=1}^{l}\phi_{h}(s_{k},\bm{a}_{k})\phi_{h}(s_{k},\bm{a}_{k})^{\top}|\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h},l\in[N],s_{k}\in\mathcal{S},\bm{a}_{k}\in\mathcal{A},\forall k\in[l]\}
be the set of all possible covariance matrix generated in the algorithm. Fix a variable
L
, for each
h\in[H]
, define a function class
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}\in\mathbb{R}^{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}
by

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}=\{

\displaystyle\left.f(s,\bm{a}):=r_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})+\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta+\min\left\{c\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma_{h}^{-1}},H\right\}\right|

\displaystyle i\in[M],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h},\|\theta\|_{2}\leq 2H^{2}\sqrt{d},c\in[0,L],\Sigma_{h}\in\mathcal{C}_{h}\}

For a given parameter
\tilde{\varepsilon}
, let
\mathcal{N}_{h}
be a
\tilde{\varepsilon}
-net of
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}
under the
\|\cdot\|_{\infty}
metric. Define
\Pi_{h}
as the set of all possible policies produced by equation
1
(or equation
2
or equation
3
, according to the problem setting). We then define the discriminator function class
\mathcal{F}_{h}
as followings:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{1,h}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\left.f(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[\left|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta-\phi_{h}^{\prime}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\prime}\right|\right]\right|\phi_{h},\phi_{h}^{\prime}\in\Phi_{h},\max\{\|\theta\|_{2},\|\theta^{\prime}\|_{2}\}\leq\sqrt{d}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{2,h}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\left.f(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]\right|i\in[M],\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1},\|\theta\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{3,h}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\left.f(s):=\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad i\in[M],\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1},\|\theta\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\bigg\},

(For NE and CCE)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{3,h}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\left.f(s):=\max_{\omega_{h+1,i}\in\Omega_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\omega_{h+1,i}\circ\pi_{h+1})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad i\in[M],\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1},\|\theta\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\bigg\},

(For CE)

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{4,h}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\left.f(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\frac{\min\left\{c\|\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma_{h+1}^{-1}},H\right\}}{H^{2}}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad c\in[0,L],\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\Sigma_{h+1}\in\mathcal{C}_{h+1},\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1},\|\theta\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}

\displaystyle:=\{f:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow[0,1]\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{h}

\displaystyle:=\left(\mathcal{F}_{1,h}\cup\mathcal{F}_{2,h}\cup\mathcal{F}_{3,h}\cup\mathcal{F}_{4,h}\right)\cap\mathcal{G}.

C.3
High Probability Events

We define the following event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}

\displaystyle:\ \forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\rho\in\left\{\rho^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}\right\},f\in\mathcal{F}_{h},\quad\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left(\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)f\right)(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}

\displaystyle:\ \forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h},\quad\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi_{h}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}}}\right)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}.

Similar to the procedure of the model-based case, we first prove a few lemmas which lead to the conclusion that
\mathcal{E}
holds with a high probability.

Lemma C.1
.

For any
n\in[N],h\in[H]
, we have
\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,\bm{a})=\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime})
for some
\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{d}
. For any function
f:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow[0,1]
and
n\in[N],h\in[H]
, we have
\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}
, and there exist
\theta,\tilde{\theta}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
such that
\left(P_{h}^{\star}f\right)(s,\bm{a})=\phi_{h}^{\star}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta,\ \left(\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}f\right)(s,\bm{a})=\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}
and
\max\{\|\theta\|_{2},\|\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}\}\leq\sqrt{d}
. Furthermore, we have
\|\tilde{\theta}\|_{\infty}\leq 1
.

Proof.

By definition, we have

\displaystyle\left(P_{h}^{\star}f\right)(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{S}}P_{h}^{\star}(s^{\prime}|s,\bm{a})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi^{\star}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\int_{\mathcal{S}}w_{h}^{\star}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi^{\star}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta,

where
\theta=\int_{\mathcal{S}}w_{h}^{\star}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}
. Furthermore, note that
\|f\|_{\infty}\leq 1
, according to the assumption on
w_{h}^{\star}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}w^{\star}_{h}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d},

which implies
\|\theta\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}
. For
\left(\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}f\right)(s,\bm{a})
, let

\displaystyle\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h}(s^{\prime}):=\left(\sum_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\in\mathcal{D}_{h}^{(n)}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{D}}_{h}^{(n)}}\phi_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\phi_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}+\lambda I_{d}\right)^{-1}\sum_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}},\tilde{s}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{D}_{h}^{(n)}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{D}}_{h}^{(n)}}\phi_{h}^{(n)}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\bm{1}_{\tilde{s}^{\prime}=s^{\prime}}.

Since
\phi_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})
is an one-hot vector, one has
\left\|\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime})\right\|_{\infty}\leq 1,\forall s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}
. It follows that
\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\leq 1
, and therefore,
\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}
. By definition, we have

\displaystyle\left(\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}f\right)(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime}|s,\bm{a})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\phi_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta},

where
\tilde{\theta}=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}
. Due to the property we just derived for
\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h}
, similar to the proof of the true model, we also have
\|\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}
. Meanwhile, one can easily see that
\|\tilde{\theta}\|_{\infty}\leq 1
, using the fact
\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{w}_{h}^{(n)}(s^{\prime})f(s^{\prime})\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\right\|_{\infty}\leq 1
.
∎

Lemma C.2

(Covering Number of
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}
)
.

When
\Phi_{h}
is the set of one-hot vectors and
\lambda\geq 1
, it’s possible to construct the
\tilde{\varepsilon}
-net
\mathcal{N}_{h}
such that
|\mathcal{N}_{h}|\leq M\left(\frac{12H^{2}L^{2}d}{\tilde{\varepsilon}}\right)^{3d}|\Phi|,\forall h\in[H]
. Furthermore, we have
|\Pi_{h}|\leq|\mathcal{N}_{h}|^{M}\leq M^{M}\left(\frac{12H^{2}L^{2}d}{\tilde{\varepsilon}}\right)^{3Md}|\Phi|^{M}
.

Proof.

Recall that

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}=\{

\displaystyle\left.f(s,\bm{a}):=r_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})+\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta+\min\{c\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma_{h}^{-1}},H\}\right|

\displaystyle i\in[M],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h},\|\theta\|_{2}\leq 2H^{2}\sqrt{d},c\in[0,L],\Sigma\in\mathcal{C}_{h}\}.

Note that when
\Phi_{h}
is the set of one-hot vectors,
\Sigma_{h}
will be a diagonal matrix. In this case,
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}
is the subset of the following function class:

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}^{\prime}:=\{

\displaystyle\left.f(s,\bm{a}):=r_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})+\min\{c\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\prime},H\}+\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right|

\displaystyle i\in[M],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h},0\leq c\leq L,\max\{\|\theta\|_{2},\|\theta^{\prime}\|_{2}\}\leq 2H^{2}\sqrt{d}\}.

Let
\Theta
be an
\ell_{2}
-cover of the set
\{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\|\theta\|_{2}\leq 2H^{2}\sqrt{d}\}
at scale
\tilde{\varepsilon}
. Then we know
|\Theta|\leq\left(\frac{4H^{2}\sqrt{d}}{\tilde{\varepsilon}}\right)^{d}
. Let
\mathcal{W}
be an
\ell_{\infty}
-cover of the set
[0,L]
at scale
\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\prime}:=\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{2H^{2}\sqrt{d}}
, we have
|\mathcal{W}|\leq\frac{2H^{2}L\sqrt{d}}{\tilde{\varepsilon}}
. Define the covering set by

\displaystyle\bar{\mathcal{F}}_{h}:=\left\{\left.\bar{f}(s,\bm{a}):=r_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})+\min\{\tilde{c}\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime},H\}+\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right|i\in[M],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h},\tilde{c}\in\mathcal{W},\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta\right\}.

Then, for any
f\in\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}^{\prime}
, by definition, suppose
f
takes the following form:

\displaystyle f(s,\bm{a}):=r_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})+\min\{c\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\prime},H\}+\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta,\quad 0\leq c\leq L,\max\{\|\theta\|_{2},\|\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}\}\leq 2H^{2}\sqrt{d}.

Then we can find
\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta
,
\tilde{c}\in\mathcal{W}
such that
\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}\leq\tilde{\varepsilon},\|\theta^{\prime}-\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\|_{2}\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}
and
|c-\tilde{c}|\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\prime}
. Let

\displaystyle\bar{f}(s,\bm{a}):=r_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})+\min\{\tilde{c}\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime},H\}+\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta},

then we have

\displaystyle|f(s,\bm{a})-\bar{f}(s,\bm{a})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{2}\left\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\right\|_{2}+\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{2}\left\|\tilde{c}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}-c\theta^{\prime}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{\varepsilon}+|\tilde{c}-c|\left\|\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}+c\left\|\theta^{\prime}-\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{\varepsilon}+2H^{2}\sqrt{d}\tilde{\varepsilon}^{\prime}+L\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3L\tilde{\varepsilon},

which implies
\bar{\mathcal{F}}_{h}
is a
3L\tilde{\varepsilon}
-covering of
\tilde{F}_{h}^{\prime}
(therefore, is a
3L\tilde{\varepsilon}
-covering of
\tilde{F}_{h}
), and we have

\displaystyle\left|\bar{\mathcal{F}}_{h}\right|\leq M\left(\frac{4H^{2}Ld}{\tilde{\varepsilon}}\right)^{3d}|\Phi|.

Replacing
\tilde{\varepsilon}
by
\frac{\tilde{\varepsilon}}{3L}
, we get an
\tilde{\varepsilon}
-covering of
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}
whose size is no larger than
M\left(\frac{12H^{2}L^{2}d}{\tilde{\varepsilon}}\right)^{3d}|\Phi|
. For
\Pi_{h}
, since each policy is determined by
M
members from
\mathcal{N}_{h}
, we have
|\Pi_{h}|\leq|\mathcal{N}_{h}|^{M}
, which has finished the proof.
∎

Lemma C.3

(Covering Number of
\mathcal{F}_{h}
)
.

When
\Phi_{h}
is the set of one-hot vectors and
\lambda\geq 1
. The
\gamma
-covering number of
\mathcal{F}_{h}
is at most
4M|\Pi_{h+1}|\left(\frac{6L^{2}d}{\gamma}\right)^{3d}|\Phi|^{2}
.

Proof.

We cover
\mathcal{F}_{1,h},\mathcal{F}_{2,h},\mathcal{F}_{3,h},\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
separately. For
\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
, let
\Theta
be an
\ell_{2}
-cover of the set
\{\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\|\theta\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}\}
at scale
\gamma
. Then we know
|\Theta|\leq\left(\frac{2\sqrt{d}}{\gamma}\right)^{d}
. Define the covering set of
\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
as

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{1,h}:=\left\{\left.\tilde{f}(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[\left|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}-\phi_{h}^{\prime}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\right|\right]\right|\phi_{h},\phi_{h}^{\prime}\in\Phi_{h},\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta\right\}.

For any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
, suppose

\displaystyle f(s)=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[\left|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta-\phi_{h}^{\prime}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\prime}\right|\right],\quad\phi_{h},\phi_{h}^{\prime}\in\Phi_{h},\max\{\|\theta\|_{2},\|\theta^{\prime}\|_{2}\}\leq\sqrt{d},

Then we can find
\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta
such that
\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}\leq\gamma,\|\theta^{\prime}-\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\|_{2}\leq\gamma
. Let

\displaystyle\tilde{f}(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[\left|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}-\phi_{h}^{\prime}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\right|\right].

Then we have

\displaystyle|f(s)-\tilde{f}(s)|\leq\frac{1}{A}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\right\|_{2}+\frac{1}{A}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\phi_{h}^{\prime}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{2}\left\|\theta^{\prime}-\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\leq 2\gamma,

which implies
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{1,h}
is a
2\gamma
covering of
\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
. Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{1,h}|\leq\left(\frac{2d}{\gamma}\right)^{2d}|\Phi|^{2}.

For
\mathcal{F}_{2,h}
, we construct

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{2,h}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\left.\tilde{f}(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]\right|i\in[M],\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1},\tilde{\theta}\in\Theta,\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1}\right\}.

Similar to the proof of
\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
, we may verify
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{2,h}
is a
\gamma
-covering of
\mathcal{F}_{2,h}
, and

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{2,h}|\leq M|\Pi_{h+1}|\left(\frac{2d}{\gamma}\right)^{d}|\Phi|.

For
\mathcal{F}_{3,h}
, we only prove the case of NE or CCE, the case of CE can be proved in a similar way. We construct

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{3,h}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\left.\tilde{f}(s):=\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]\right|

\displaystyle i\in[M],\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1},\tilde{\theta}\in\Theta,\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1}\bigg\}.

For any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{3,h}
, suppose

\displaystyle f(s)=\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right],\quad i\in[M],\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1},\|\theta\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}.

Then we can find
\tilde{\theta}\in\Theta
such that
\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}\leq\gamma
. Let

\displaystyle\tilde{f}(s)=\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right],

we have

\displaystyle f(s)-\tilde{f}(s)=

\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]

\displaystyle-\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta-\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\gamma,

and

\displaystyle\tilde{f}(s)-f(s)=

\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle-\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\bigg(\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]

\displaystyle-\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\tilde{\mu}_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i})(s)}\left[\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}-\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\gamma,

which implies

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{f}(s)-f(s)\right|\leq\gamma.

Therefore, we conclude
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{3,h}
is a
\gamma
-covering of
\mathcal{F}_{3,h}
, and

\displaystyle|\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{3,h}|\leq M|\Pi_{h+1}|\left(\frac{2d}{\gamma}\right)^{d}|\Phi|.

For
\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
, note that when
\Phi_{h}
is the set of one-hot vectors,
\Sigma_{h}
will be a diagonal matrix. In this case,
\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
is the subset of the following function class:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{4,h}^{\prime}:=\bigg\{

\displaystyle\left.f(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\frac{\min\{c\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\prime},H\}}{H^{2}}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right]\right|

\displaystyle 0\leq c\leq L,\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\max\{\|\theta\|_{2},\|\theta^{\prime}\|_{2}\}\leq\sqrt{d},\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1}\bigg\}.

In this case, let
\mathcal{W}
be an
\ell_{\infty}
cover of the set
[0,L]
at scale
\tilde{\gamma}:=\frac{\gamma}{\sqrt{d}}
, we have
|\mathcal{W}|\leq\frac{L\sqrt{d}}{\gamma}
. Let

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{4,h}:=\bigg\{

\displaystyle\left.\tilde{f}(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\frac{\min\{\tilde{c}\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime},H\}}{H^{2}}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\tilde{c}\in\mathcal{W},\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta,\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1}\bigg\}.

Then, for any
f\in\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
, suppose

\displaystyle f(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}

\displaystyle\left[\frac{\min\{c\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\prime},H\}}{H^{2}}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta\right],

\displaystyle 0\leq c\leq L,\pi_{h+1}\in\Pi_{h+1},\max\{\|\theta\|_{2},\|\theta^{\prime}\|_{2}\}\leq\sqrt{d},\phi_{h+1}\in\Phi_{h+1}.

Then we can find
\tilde{\theta},\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\in\Theta
,
\tilde{c}\in\mathcal{W}
such that
\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\|_{2}\leq\gamma,\|\theta^{\prime}-\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\|_{2}\leq\gamma
and
|c-\tilde{c}|\leq\tilde{\gamma}
. Let

\displaystyle\tilde{f}(s):=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\frac{\min\{\tilde{c}\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime},H\}}{H^{2}}+\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\tilde{\theta}\right],

then we have

\displaystyle|f(s)-\tilde{f}(s)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\|\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})\|_{2}\left\|\theta-\tilde{\theta}\right\|_{2}\right]+\frac{1}{H^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h+1}(s)}\left[\|\phi_{h+1}(s,\bm{a})\|_{2}\left\|\tilde{c}\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}-c\theta^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\gamma+\frac{1}{H^{2}}\left(|\tilde{c}-c|\left\|\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}+c\left\|\theta^{\prime}-\tilde{\theta}^{\prime}\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\gamma+\frac{\sqrt{d}}{H^{2}}\tilde{\gamma}+\frac{L}{H^{2}}\gamma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3L\gamma,

which implies
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{4,h}
is a
3L\gamma
-covering of
\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
, and we have

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{4,h}\right|\leq|\Pi_{h+1}|\left(\frac{2Ld}{\gamma}\right)^{3d}|\Phi|.

In summary, we know
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}:=\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{1,h}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{2,h}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{3,h}\cup\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{4,h}
is a
3L\gamma
-covering of
\mathcal{F}_{h}
. And

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{F}_{h}\right|\leq 4M|\Pi_{h+1}|\left(\frac{2Ld}{\gamma}\right)^{3d}|\Phi|^{2}.

Replacing
\gamma
by
\frac{\gamma}{3L}
, we get an
\gamma
-covering of
\mathcal{F}_{h}
whose size is no larger than
4M|\Pi_{h+1}|\left(\frac{6L^{2}d}{\gamma}\right)^{3d}|\Phi|^{2}
, which has finished the proof.
∎

Below we omit the superscript
n
and subscript
h
when clear from the context. Denote

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\lambda,\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\theta,f)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}|}\sum_{(s,\bm{a},s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{D}}\left(\phi(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta-f(s^{\prime})\right)^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{|\mathcal{D}|}\|\theta\|^{2}_{2}

(14)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\theta,f)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|\mathcal{D}|}\sum_{(s,\bm{a},s^{\prime})\in\mathcal{D}}\left(\phi(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta-f(s^{\prime})\right)^{2}

(15)

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho,s^{\prime}\sim P^{\star}(s,\bm{a})}\left[\left(\phi(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta-f(s^{\prime})\right)^{2}\right].

(16)

Lemma C.4

(Uniform Convergence for Square Loss)
.

Let there be a dataset
\mathcal{D}:=\{(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i},s^{\prime}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
collected in
n
episodes. Denote the data generating distribution in iteration
i
by
d_{i}
, and
\rho=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}d_{i}
. Note that
d_{i}
can depend on the randomness in episodes
1,\ldots,i-1
. For a finite feature class
\Phi
and a discriminator class
\mathcal{F}:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow[0,1]
with
\gamma
-covering number
\|\mathcal{F}\|_{\gamma}
, with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\left|\left[\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right]-\left[\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right]+\frac{64\log(\frac{2(4n)^{d}\cdot|\Phi|\cdot\|\mathcal{F}\|_{1/2n}}{\delta})}{n}

for all
\phi\in\Phi
,
\|\theta\|_{\infty}\leq 1
and
f\in\mathcal{F}
. Recall that
\phi^{\star}
is the true feature and
\theta^{\star}_{f}
is defined as
\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P^{\star}(s,\bm{a})}[f(s^{\prime})]=\langle\phi^{\star}(s,\bm{a}),\theta_{f}^{\star}\rangle
.

Proof.

To start, we focus on a given
f\in\mathcal{F}
.
We first give a high probability bound on the following deviation term:

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{*},\theta^{*}_{f},f)-\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{*},\theta^{*}_{f},f)\right)\right|.

Denote
g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})=\phi(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})^{\top}\theta
and
g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})=\phi^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})^{\top}\theta^{\star}_{f}
. At episode
i
, let
\mathcal{F}_{i-1}
be the
\sigma
-field generated by all the random variables over the first
i-1
episodes, for the random variable
Y_{i}:=\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)^{2}-\left(g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)^{2}-\left(g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})+g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-2f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right].

Here the conditional expectation is taken according to the distribution
d_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}
. The last equality is due to the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s_{i},\bm{a}_{i}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}_{i}}\left[\left(g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})\right)|s_{i},\bm{a}_{i}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0.

Next, for the conditional variance of the random variable, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{V}[Y_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[Y_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})+g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-2f(s^{\prime}_{i})\right)^{2}\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16\mathbb{E}\left[\left(g(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})-g^{\star}(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}].

Noticing
Y_{i}\in[-4,4]
. Applying Lemma 1 in
[
Foster and Rakhlin 2020
]
, we get with probability at least
1-\delta^{\prime}
, we can bound the deviation term above as:

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)-\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{V}[Y_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n^{2}}}+\frac{16\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{3n}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{32\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n^{2}}}+\frac{16\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{3n},

Further, consider a finite point-wise cover of the function class
\mathcal{G}:=\{g(s,\bm{a})=\phi(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta:\phi\in\Phi,\|\theta\|_{\infty}\leq 1\}
. Note that, with a
\ell_{\infty}
-cover
\overline{\mathcal{W}}
of
\mathcal{W}=\{\|\theta\|_{\infty}\leq 1\}
at scale
\gamma
, we have for all
(s,\bm{a})
and
\phi\in\Phi
, there exists
\bar{\theta}\in\overline{\mathcal{W}}
,
|\langle\phi(s,\bm{a}),\theta-\bar{\theta}\rangle|\leq\gamma
, and we have
|\mathcal{W}|=\left(\frac{2}{\gamma}\right)^{d}
.
Let
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}
be a
\gamma
-covering set of
\mathcal{F}
. For any
f\in\mathcal{F}
, there exists
\bar{f}\in\tilde{\mathcal{F}}
such that
\|f-\bar{f}\|_{\infty}\leq\gamma
. Then, applying a union bound over elements in
\Phi\times\overline{\mathcal{W}}\times\tilde{\mathcal{F}}
, with probability
1-|\Phi||\overline{\mathcal{W}}||\tilde{\mathcal{F}}|\delta^{\prime}
, for all
\theta\in\mathcal{W}
,
f\in\mathcal{F}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)-\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\bar{\theta},\bar{f})-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{\bar{f}},\bar{f})-\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\bar{\theta},\bar{f})-\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{\bar{f}},\bar{f})\right)\right|+16\gamma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{32\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{Y}_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n^{2}}}+\frac{16\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{3n}+16\gamma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\bar{Y}_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]+\frac{16\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n}+\frac{16\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{3n}+16\gamma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[Y_{i}|\mathcal{F}_{i-1}]+\frac{16\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n}+\frac{16\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{3n}+32\gamma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right)+\frac{32\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n}+32\gamma

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right)+\frac{64\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}}{n}

(setting
\gamma=1/n
)

where
\bar{Y}_{i}:=\left(\phi(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})^{\top}\bar{\theta}-\bar{f}(s^{\prime})\right)^{2}-\left(\phi(s_{i},\bm{a}_{i})^{\top}\theta^{\star}_{\bar{f}}-\bar{f}(s^{\prime})\right)^{2}
. Finally, setting
\delta=\delta^{\prime}/\left(|\Phi||\overline{\mathcal{W}}||\tilde{\mathcal{F}}|\right)
, we get
\log\frac{2}{\delta^{\prime}}\leq\log\frac{2(4n)^{d}|\Phi||\tilde{\mathcal{F}}|}{\delta}
. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma C.5

(Deviation Bounds for Representation Learning in Alg.
2
)
.

Let
\varepsilon^{\prime}=\frac{128\log(\frac{2(4n)^{d}\cdot|\Phi|\cdot\|\mathcal{F}\|_{1/2n}}{\delta})}{n}
. If the representation learning module in Alg.
2
is called with a dataset
\mathcal{D}
of size
n
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
, for any
f\in\mathcal{F}\subset[0,1]^{\mathcal{S}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left(\hat{\phi}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{f}-\phi^{\star}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\star}_{f}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon^{\prime}+\frac{2\lambda d}{n}.

Proof.

We begin by using the result in Lemma
C.4
such that, with probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
\|\theta\|_{\infty}\leq 1
,
\phi\in\Phi
and
f\in\mathcal{F}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\left[\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right]-\left[\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right]\right|\leq\frac{1}{2}\left[\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi,\theta,f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right]+\varepsilon^{\prime}/2.

Thus, with probability at least
1-\delta
we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left(\hat{\phi}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{f}-\phi^{\star}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\star}_{f}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\hat{\phi},\hat{\theta}_{f},f)-\mathcal{L}_{\rho}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)

(since
\mathbb{E}_{s^{\prime}\sim P^{\star}(s,\bm{a})}\left[f(s^{\prime})\right]=\phi^{\star}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\star}_{f}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\hat{\phi},\hat{\theta}_{f},f)-\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)\right)+\varepsilon^{\prime}

(Lemma
C.4
, and
\|\hat{\theta}_{f}\|_{\infty}\leq 1
according to the proof in Lemma
C.1
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(\mathcal{L}_{\lambda,\mathcal{D}}(\hat{\phi},\hat{\theta}_{f},f)-\mathcal{L}_{\lambda,\mathcal{D}}(\phi^{\star},\theta^{\star}_{f},f)+\frac{\lambda}{n}\|\theta^{\star}_{f}\|^{2}_{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\prime}+\frac{2\lambda d}{n},

(by the optimality of
\hat{\phi},\hat{\theta}_{f}
under
\mathcal{L}_{\lambda,\mathcal{D}}(\cdot,\cdot,f)
)

which means the inequality in the lemma statement holds. Here, we use
\|\theta^{\star}_{f}\|^{2}_{2}\leq d
.
∎

Lemma C.6
.

When
\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}
is computed using Alg.
2
and the Markov games is a block Markov game, if we set

\displaystyle\lambda

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(d\log\frac{NH|\Phi|}{\delta}\right),\ \zeta^{(n)}=\Theta\left(\frac{d^{2}M\log\frac{dNHML|\Phi|}{\delta\tilde{\varepsilon}}}{n}\right).

then
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Proof.

Combining Lemma
C.5
and Lemma
C.3
, we have that

\displaystyle\max_{f\in\mathcal{F}_{h}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[(\hat{\phi}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\hat{\theta}_{f}-\phi^{\star}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta^{\star}_{f})^{2}\right]\leq\varepsilon^{\prime}+\frac{2\lambda d}{n}\leq\zeta^{(n)}:=\Theta\left(d^{2}M\frac{\log\left(\frac{dNHML|\Phi|}{\delta\tilde{\varepsilon}}\right)}{n}\right),

which shows
\mathcal{E}_{1}
holds with a high probability. Combining this result with Lemma
E.1
, we have proved Lemma
C.6
.
∎

C.4
Statistical Guarantees

To ensure the algorithm is well-defined, we first prove the following lemma which implies the optimistic Q-value estimators always belong to the function class
\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}
.

Lemma C.7
.

When
\alpha^{(n)}\leq L
, we have
\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\in\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h},\forall h\in[H],i\in[M],n\in[N]
.

Proof.

Because
\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}
is upper bounded by
H
, by induction one can easily get
\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\leq 2H^{2}
. Then according to the result of Lemma
C.1
, we know
(\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i})(s,a)=\phi_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\theta
with
\|\theta\|_{2}\leq 2H^{2}\sqrt{d}
. We conclude
\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\in\tilde{\mathcal{F}}_{h}
.
∎

We will show later that our choice of
\alpha^{(n)}
and
L
always satisfies the condition
\alpha^{(n)}\leq L
.

Lemma C.8
.

We have

•

For NE and CCE,

\displaystyle\max_{\pi_{h,i}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h,i},\pi_{h,-i}^{(n)}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)\leq\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}^{(n)}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)+2\tilde{\varepsilon};

•

For CE,

\displaystyle\max_{\omega_{h,i}\in\Omega_{h,i}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\omega_{h,i}\circ\pi_{h}^{(n)}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)\leq\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}^{(n)}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)+2\tilde{\varepsilon}.

Proof.

We only prove the case of NE and CCE, the case of CE can be proved similarly. Let
\tilde{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}
be the nearest neighbour of
\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}
in
\mathcal{N}_{h}
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{\pi_{h,i}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h,i},\pi_{h,-i}^{(n)}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\pi_{h,i}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h,i},\pi_{h,-i}^{(n)}}\tilde{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)+\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}^{(n)}}\tilde{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)+\tilde{\varepsilon}

(Definition of
\pi_{h}^{(n)}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}^{(n)}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)+2\tilde{\varepsilon},

which has finished the proof.
∎

Lemma C.9

(One-step back inequality for the learned model)
.

Suppose the event
\mathcal{E}
holds. Consider a set of functions
\{g_{h}\}^{H}_{h=1}
that satisfies
g_{h}\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, s.t.
\|g_{h}\|_{\infty}\leq B
. For a given policy
\pi
, suppose
\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[g_{h}(\cdot,\bm{a})\right]\in\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
, then we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]},\quad h=1\\
&\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d+nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}},B\right\}\right],\quad h\geq 2\end{aligned}\right.

Recall
\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}=n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d}
.

Proof.

For step
h=1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[g_{1}(s,\bm{a})\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1},\bm{a}\sim\pi_{1}(s)}\left[g_{1}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\max_{(s,\bm{a})}\frac{d_{1}(s)\pi_{1}(\bm{a}|s)}{\rho^{(n)}_{1}(s,\bm{a})}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},\bm{a}^{\prime})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s^{\prime},\bm{a}^{\prime})\right]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\max_{(s,\bm{a})}\frac{d_{1}(s)\pi_{1}(\bm{a}|s)}{d_{1}(s)u_{\mathcal{A}}(\bm{a})}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},\bm{a}^{\prime})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s^{\prime},\bm{a}^{\prime})\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]}.

For step
h=2,\ldots,H-1
, we observe the following one-step-back decomposition:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1},s\sim\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h}(s)}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s,B\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}},B\right\}\right].

where we use the fact that
g_{h}
is bounded by
B
. Then,

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s\right)^{\top}\left(n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s,\bm{a})\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d}\right)\left(\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)^{\top}\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d

(
\left\|\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\infty}\leq B
and by Lemma
C.1

\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)l(s)\mathrm{d}s\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}
for any
l:\mathcal{S}\to[0,1]
.)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h}(s)}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d

(Importance sampling)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{s\sim P_{h-1}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d+nA^{2}\xi^{(n)}

(Assumption on
g_{h}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},s\sim P_{h-1}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d+nA^{2}\xi^{(n)}.

(Jensen)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d+nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}.

(Definition of
\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}
)

Combing the above results together, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}\in\mathcal{A}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1}(s)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}|s)g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\mathrm{d}s\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}},B\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d+nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}},B\right\}\right],

which has finished the proof.
∎

The following lemma is an exact copy of Lemma
B.4
, and here we state it again just for completeness.

Lemma C.10

(One-step back inequality for the true model)
.

Consider a set of functions
\{g_{h}\}^{H}_{h=1}
that satisfies
g_{h}\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, s.t.
\|g_{h}\|_{\infty}\leq B
. Then for any given policy
\pi
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[g_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]},\quad h=1\\
&\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h-1}}\left[\left\|\phi^{\star}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1},\phi^{\star}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{nA\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s,\bm{a})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d},\quad h\geq 2\end{aligned}\right.

Recall
\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}=n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\gamma^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\phi_{h}^{\star}(s,\bm{a})\phi_{h}^{\star}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d}
.

Lemma C.11

(Optimism for NE and CCE)
.

Consider an episode
n\in[N]
and set
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\sqrt{nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\right)
. When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds and the policy
\pi^{(n)}
is computed by solving NE or CCE, we have

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}(s)-v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}(s)\geq-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}-2H\tilde{\varepsilon},\quad\forall n\in[N],i\in[M].

Proof.

Denote
\tilde{\mu}_{h,i}^{(n)}(\cdot|s):=\argmax_{\mu}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mu,\pi_{h,-i}^{(n)}}Q^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}\right)(s)
and let
\tilde{\pi}_{h}^{(n)}=\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}_{h,i}\times\pi_{h,-i}^{(n)}
. Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left|\frac{1}{H}\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V_{h+1,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{(n)}}\right|(s,\bm{a})
, note that by definition, we have
\frac{1}{H}V_{h+1,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{(n)}}(s)
is bounded by
1
, and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{H}V_{h+1,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{(n)}}(s)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\tilde{\pi}_{h}^{(n)}(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\frac{1}{H}\left(P_{h+1}^{\star}V_{h+2,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{(n)}}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{\mu_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\mu_{h+1,i}\times\pi_{h+1,-i}^{(n)})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\frac{1}{H}\left(P_{h+1}^{\star}V_{h+2,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{(n)}}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]\in\mathcal{F}_{3,h}.

where we use the result of Lemma
C.1
and get
\frac{1}{H}\left(P_{h+1}^{\star}V_{h+2,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{(n)}}\right)(s,\bm{a})
is a linear function in
\phi^{\star}_{h+1}
and the 2-norm of the weight is upper bounded by
\sqrt{d}
. Then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H]

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi_{h}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h}.

A direct conclusion of the event
\mathcal{E}
is we can find an absolute constant
c
, such that

\displaystyle\beta_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}},H\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H].

Next, we prove by induction that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2(H-h+1)\tilde{\varepsilon},\quad\forall h\in[H].

(17)

First, notice that
\forall h\in[H]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}(s)\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi^{(n)}_{h}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)-\left(\mathbb{D}_{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}_{h}}Q^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\mathbb{D}_{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}_{h}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)-\left(\mathbb{D}_{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}_{h}}Q^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}\right)(s)\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Q^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon},

where the inequality uses the result of Lemma
C.8
. Now we are ready to prove equation
17
,

•

When
h=H
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{H,i}(s)\right]\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Q^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{H,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}.

•

Suppose the statement is true for
h+1
, then for step
h
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Q^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)(s,\bm{a})-\left(P^{\star}_{h}V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2(H-h+1)\tilde{\varepsilon},

where the last row uses the induction assumption.

Therefore, we have proved equation
17
. We then apply
h=1
to equation
17
, and get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}.

For the second term, since
\frac{1}{H}\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}
is linear in
\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}
and
\frac{1}{H}P_{h}^{\star}V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}
is linear in
\phi_{h}^{\star}
, and according to the result of Lemma
C.1
, the 2-norm of their weights are both upper bounded by
\sqrt{d}
. Therefore, we have
\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(\cdot,\bm{a})\right]\in\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
. By Lemma
C.9
, we have for
h=1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho_{1}^{(n)}}\left[\left(f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]}\leq\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}.

And
\forall h\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]+d\lambda+nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}},1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda},1\right\}\right].

Note that we here use
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\leq 1,\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
and
\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
. Then according to our choice of
\alpha^{(n)}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}},1\right\}\right].

Combining all things together,

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\}\right]-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}-2H\tilde{\varepsilon},

which proves the inequality.
∎

Lemma C.12

(Optimism for CE)
.

Consider an episode
n\in[N]
and set
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\sqrt{nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\right)
. When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds, we have

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}(s)\geq-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}-2H\tilde{\varepsilon},\quad\forall n\in[N],i\in[M].

Proof.

Denote
