\tilde{\omega}_{h,i}^{(n)}=\argmax_{\omega_{h}\in\Omega_{h,i}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\omega_{h}\circ\pi_{h}^{(n)}}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}Q_{h,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}\right)(s)
and let
\tilde{\pi}_{h}^{(n)}=\tilde{\omega}_{h,i}\circ\pi^{(n)}_{h}
. Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left|\frac{1}{H}\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V_{h+1,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}\right|(s,\bm{a})
, note that by definition, we have
\frac{1}{H}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V_{h+1,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}(s)
is bounded by
1
, and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{H}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V_{h+1,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}(s)=\max_{\omega_{h+1,i}\in\Omega_{h+1,i}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim(\omega_{h+1,i}\circ\pi_{h})(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\frac{1}{H}\left(P_{h+1}^{\star}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V_{h+2,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]\in\mathcal{F}_{3,h}.

where we use the result of Lemma
C.1
and get
\frac{1}{H}\left(P_{h+1}^{\star}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V_{h+2,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}\right)(s,\bm{a})
is a linear function in
\phi^{\star}_{h}
and the 2-norm of the weight is upper bounded by
\sqrt{d}
. Then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H]

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi_{h}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h}.

A direct conclusion of the event
\mathcal{E}
is we can find an absolute constant
c
, such that

\displaystyle\beta_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}},H\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H].

Next, we prove by induction that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V_{h,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2(H-h+1)\tilde{\varepsilon},\quad\forall h\in[H].

(18)

First, notice that
\forall h\in[H]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V_{h,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}(s)\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi^{(n)}_{h}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)-\left(\mathbb{D}_{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}_{h}}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}Q^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\mathbb{D}_{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}_{h}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)-\left(\mathbb{D}_{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}_{h}}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}Q^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}\right)(s)\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}Q^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}.

where the inequality uses the result of Lemma
C.8
. Now we are ready to prove equation
18
,

•

When
h=H
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{H,i}(s)\right]\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}Q^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{H,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}.

•

Suppose the statement is true for
h+1
, then for step
h
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}Q^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)(s,\bm{a})-\left(P^{\star}_{h}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})-\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]-2\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2(H-h+1)\tilde{\varepsilon},

where the last row uses the induction assumption.

Therefore, we have proved equation
18
. We then apply
h=1
to equation
18
, and get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Hf^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}.

For the second term, since
\frac{1}{H}\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}
is linear in
\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}
and
\frac{1}{H}P_{h}^{\star}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}
is linear in
\phi_{h}^{\star}
, and according to the result of Lemma
C.1
, the 2-norm of their weights are both upper bounded by
\sqrt{d}
. Therefore, we have
\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim U(\mathcal{A})}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(\cdot,\bm{a})\right]\in\mathcal{F}_{1,h}
. By Lemma
C.9
, we have for
h=1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho_{1}^{(n)}}\left[\left(f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]}\leq\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}.

And
\forall h\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]+d\lambda+nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}},1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}}\sqrt{nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda},1\right\}\right].

Note that we here use
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\leq 1,\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
and
\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
. Then according to our choice of
\alpha^{(n)}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}},1\right\}\right].

Combining all things together,

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\}\right]-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}-2H\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}-2H\tilde{\varepsilon},

which proves the inequality.
∎

Lemma C.13

(pessimism)
.

Consider an episode
n\in[N]
and set
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\sqrt{nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\right)
. When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds, we have

\displaystyle\underline{v}_{i}^{(n)}(s)-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}(s)\leq H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},\quad\forall n\in[N],i\in[M].

Proof.

Let
\tilde{f}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left|\frac{1}{H}\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V_{h+1,i}^{\pi^{(n)}}\right|(s,\bm{a})
, note that by definition, we have
\frac{1}{H}V_{h+1,i}^{\pi^{(n)}}(s)
is bounded by
1
, and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{H}V_{h+1,i}^{\pi^{(n)}}(s)=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h}^{(n)}(s)}\left[\frac{r_{h+1,i}(s,\bm{a})}{H}+\frac{1}{H}\left(P_{h+1}^{\star}V_{h+2,i}^{\pi^{(n)}}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]\in\mathcal{F}_{2,h}.

where we use the result of Lemma
C.1
and get
\frac{1}{H}\left(P_{h+1}^{\star}V_{h+2,i}^{\pi^{(n)}}\right)(s,\bm{a})
is a linear function in
\phi^{\star}_{h+1}
and the 2-norm of the weight is upper bounded by
\sqrt{d}
. Then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H]

\displaystyle\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi_{h}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h}.

A direct conclusion of the event
\mathcal{E}
is we can find an absolute constant
c
, such that

\displaystyle\beta_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}},H\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H].

Again, we prove the following inequality by induction:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s)\right]\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+Hf^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right],\quad\forall h\in[H].

(19)

•

When
h=H
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{V}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{H,i}(s)\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{Q}_{H,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Q^{\pi^{(n)}}_{H,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},H}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+Hf_{H}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

•

Suppose the statement is true for
h+1
, then for step
h
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-Q^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)(s,\bm{a})-\left(P^{\star}_{h}V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\left(\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+Hf^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h+1}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h+1,i}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+Hf^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})\right].

where the last row uses the induction assumption.

The remaining steps are exactly the same as the proof in Lemma
C.11
or Lemma
C.12
, we may prove

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[\min\left\{f_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]\leq\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1}}^{-1}},1\right\}\right],\quad\forall h\geq 2.

Combining all things together, we get

\displaystyle\underline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\underline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+Hf^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}^{-1}},H\right\}\right]+H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},

which has finished the proof.
∎

Lemma C.14
.

For the model-free algorithm, suppose
N
is large enough, when we pick
\lambda=\Theta\left(d\log\frac{NH|\Phi|}{\delta}\right)
,
\zeta^{(n)}=\Theta\left(\frac{d^{2}M}{n}\log\frac{dNHML|\Phi|}{\tilde{\varepsilon}\delta}\right)
,
L=\Theta(NHAMd)
,
\tilde{\varepsilon}=\frac{1}{2HN}
and
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\sqrt{nA^{2}\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\right)
, with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim H^{3}d^{2}A^{\frac{3}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{dNHAM|\Phi|}{\delta}.

Proof.

With our choice of
\lambda
and
\zeta^{(n)}
, according to Lemma
C.6
, we know
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability
1-\delta
. Furthermore, with a proper choice of the absolute constants, we have

\displaystyle\alpha^{(n)}=

\displaystyle\Theta\left(H\sqrt{d^{2}A^{2}M\log\frac{dNHML|\Phi|}{\delta}+d^{2}\log\frac{NH|\Phi|}{\delta}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(HdA\sqrt{M\log\frac{dNHMA|\Phi|}{\delta}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(NHAMd\right)\leq L.

Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\frac{1}{2H^{2}}\left|\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)\right|(s,\bm{a})
. We first verify
\frac{1}{2H^{2}}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)\in\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
. By definition, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2H^{2}}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h}^{(n)}(s)}\left[\frac{1}{H^{2}}\hat{\beta}_{h+1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\frac{1}{2H^{2}}P^{\star}_{h+1}\left(\overline{V}_{h+2,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+2,i}^{(n)}\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

The first term is equal to
\frac{1}{H^{2}}\min\left(\alpha^{(n)}\sqrt{\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h}\right)^{-1}\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})},H\right)
, which is exactly the same as that in the definition of
\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
(note that we use the property
\alpha^{(n)}\leq L,\forall n\in[N]
). For the second term, note that we have
0\leq\frac{1}{2H^{2}}\left(\overline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)\leq 1,\forall h
. Therefore, by Lemma
C.1
,
\frac{1}{2H^{2}}P^{\star}_{h+1}\left(\overline{V}_{h+2,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+2,i}^{(n)}\right)(s,\bm{a})
is a linear function in
\phi^{\star}_{h+1}
whose weight’s 2-norm is upper bounded by
\sqrt{d}
. Combing the above arguments, we conclude
\frac{1}{2H^{2}}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)\in\mathcal{F}_{4,h}
. According to the definition of the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi_{h}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\phi_{h}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],\phi_{h}\in\Phi_{h},h\in[H].

(20)

By definition, we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n)}=\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}\right\}+2H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}.

For each fixed
i\in[M],h\in[H]
and
n\in[N]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi^{(n)}_{h}}\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)-\left(\mathbb{D}_{\pi^{(n)}_{h}}\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}\right)(s)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[2\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[2\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})+\left(\left(\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}-P^{\star}_{h}\right)\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{(n)}\right)\right)(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h+1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[2\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})+2H^{2}f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]+\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h+1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h+1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ldots

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h^{\prime}}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a})+H^{2}f_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right],

where the last inequality is calculated using induction. In particular,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)\right]\leq 2\underbrace{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]}_{(a)}+2H^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]}_{(b)}.

(21)

First, we calculate the first term (a) in Inequality equation
21
. Following Lemma
C.10
and noting the bonus
\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}
is
O(H)
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\min\left(\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}},H\right)\right]

(From equation
20
)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{{nA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left\|\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}}\right]+H^{2}d\lambda}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sqrt{A\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[\left\|\hat{\phi}_{1}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{1},\hat{\phi}_{1}^{(n)}}}\right]}.

Note that we use the fact that
B=H
when applying Lemma
D.3
. In addition, we have

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left\|\hat{\phi}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\textrm{Tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]\left(n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d.

Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sqrt{{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}/n}.

Second, we calculate the term (b) in inequality equation
24
. Following Lemma
D.3
and noting
\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}
is upper-bounded by
1
(i.e.,
B=1
in Lemma
D.3
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{nA\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]+d\lambda}+\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{1}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{nA\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi_{n}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},

where in the second inequality, we use
\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho_{h}^{(n)}}\left[\left(f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
, and in the last line, recall
\sqrt{nA\zeta^{(n)}+d\lambda}\lesssim\alpha^{(n)}/H
. Then, by combining the above calculation of the term (a) and term (b) in inequality equation
24
, we have:

\displaystyle\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}\right).

Taking maximum over
i
on both sides and use the definition of
\Delta^{(n)}
, we get

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n)}=

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}\right\}+2H\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\|\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi^{\star}_{h}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}\right).

Hereafter, we take the dominating term out. Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\leq\sqrt{N\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\phi_{h}^{\star}}\phi_{h}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right]}

(CS inequality)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{N\left(\log\det\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}[\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\phi^{\star}_{h}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}]\right)-\log\det(\lambda I_{d})\right)}

(Lemma
E.2
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{dN\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}.

(Potential function bound, Lemma
E.3
noting
\|\phi_{h}^{\star}(s,\bm{a})\|_{2}\leq 1
for any
(s,\bm{a})
.)

Finally,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim

\displaystyle H\left(\sqrt{dN\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d}\right)}\sqrt{{dA\left(\alpha^{(N)}\right)^{2}}+H^{2}d\lambda}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sqrt{\frac{dA\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{3}\left(\frac{1}{H}\sqrt{dN\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}\right)+2HN\tilde{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle H^{2}d\sqrt{NA\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}

(Some algebra. We take the dominating term out. Note that
\alpha^{(n)}
is increasing in
n
)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle H^{3}d^{2}A^{\frac{3}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{dNHAM|\Phi|}{\delta}.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
4.2

Proof.

For any fixed episode
n
and agent
i
, by Lemma
C.11
, Lemma
C.12
and Lemma
C.13
, we have

\displaystyle v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right)\leq\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}+2\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon}\leq\Delta^{(n)}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon}.

Taking maximum over
i
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right\}\right)\leq\Delta^{(n)}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon}.

(22)

From Lemma
C.14
, with probability
1-\delta
, we can ensure

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}(\Delta^{(n)}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon})\lesssim H^{3}d^{2}A^{\frac{3}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}M^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{dNHAM|\Phi|}{\delta}.

Therefore, according to Lemma
E.4
, when we pick
N
to be

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{H^{6}d^{4}A^{3}M}{\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{HdAM|\Phi|}{\delta\varepsilon}\right)\right),

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}(\Delta^{(n)}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon})\leq\varepsilon.

On the other hand, from equation
22
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\hat{\pi}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\hat{\pi}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\hat{\pi}}_{i}-v^{\hat{\pi}}_{i}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n^{\star})}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n^{\star})}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon}=\min_{n\in[N]}\Delta^{(n)}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon}\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}(\Delta^{(n)}+2H\tilde{\varepsilon})\leq\varepsilon,

which has finished the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Analysis of the Factored Markov Games

In this part, we adopt the same notations as in the proof of the model-based case, including
\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h},\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h},\overline{Q}^{(n)}_{h},\underline{Q}^{(n)}_{h}
.

D.1
High Probability Events

Define the set
\bar{\Phi}_{h,i}=\{\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a}):=\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\phi_{h,j}(s[Z_{j}],\bm{a}_{j})|\phi_{h,j}\in\Phi_{h,j}\}
. Let
|\Phi|=\max_{h,j}|\Phi_{h,j}|
and
|\bar{\Phi}|=\max_{h,i}|\bar{\Phi}_{h,i}|
. Clearly, we have
|\bar{\Phi}|\leq|\Phi|^{L}
.
Define the following event

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}

\displaystyle:\ \forall n\in[N],h\in[H],i\in[M],\rho\in\left\{\rho^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}\right\},\quad\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})-P^{\star}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right\|_{1}^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}

\displaystyle:\ \forall n\in[N],h\in[H],i\in[M],\bar{\phi}_{h,i}\in\bar{\Phi}_{h,i},\quad\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}}\right)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}.

The following lemma shows that the event
\mathcal{E}
holds with a high probability with proper choices of the parameters.

Lemma D.1
.

When
\hat{P}_{h}^{(n)}
is computed using Alg.
1
with the factored setting, if we set

\displaystyle\lambda=\Theta\left(Ld^{L}\log\frac{NHM|\Phi|}{\delta}\right),\ \zeta^{(n)}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{n}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HNM}{\delta}\right),

then
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability at least
1-\delta
.

The proof of Lemma
D.1
is follows a similar procedure as that of Lemma
B.2
, with minor changes on the notations as well as some modifications on the union bound.

D.2
Statistical Guarantees

Lemma D.2

(One-step back inequality for the learned model)
.

Suppose the event
\mathcal{E}
holds. Consider a set of functions
\{g_{h}\}^{H}_{h=1}
that satisfies
g_{h}\in\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]\times\mathcal{A}_{i}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, s.t.
\|g_{h}\|_{\infty}\leq B
. For a given policy
\pi
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\sqrt{\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]},\hskip 9.24994pth=1\\
&\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\sqrt{n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}+nB^{2}\zeta^{(n)}},B\right\}\right],\hskip 9.24994pth\geq 2\end{aligned}\right.

where
\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a}):=\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s[Z_{j}],\bm{a}_{j})
, and
\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}=n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d^{|Z_{i}|}}
.

Proof.

For step
h=1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[g_{1}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1},\bm{a}_{i}\sim\pi_{1}(s)}\left[g_{1}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\max_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})}\frac{d_{1}(s)\pi_{1}(\bm{a}_{i}|s)}{\rho^{(n)}_{1}(s,\bm{a}_{i})}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},\bm{a}_{i}^{\prime})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s^{\prime}[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}^{\prime})\right]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\max_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})}\frac{d_{1}(s)\pi_{1}(\bm{a}_{i}|s)}{d_{1}(s)u_{\mathcal{A}}(\bm{a}_{i})}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},\bm{a}^{\prime}_{i})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s^{\prime}[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}^{\prime})\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]}.

For
h\geq 2
, we observe the following one-step-back decomposition:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{i})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1},s\sim\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}_{i}\sim\pi_{h}(s)}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{S}}\prod_{j=1}^{M}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})^{\top}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right]\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\pi_{h}(\bm{a}_{i}|s)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\int_{\mathcal{S}}\prod_{j=1}^{M}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})^{\top}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right]\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\pi_{h}(\bm{a}_{i}|s)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s,B\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\prod_{j=1}^{M}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})^{\top}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right]\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s,B\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\int_{\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]}\prod_{j\in Z_{i}}\left[\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})^{\top}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right]\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}],B\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\int_{\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]}\left[\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})\right]^{\top}\left[\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right]\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}],B\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\int_{\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]}\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}\left[\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right]\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}],B\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\Bigg[\min\Bigg\{\tilde{A}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt,B\Bigg\}\Bigg].

Then,

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1},i}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\int_{\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}}\prod_{j\in Z_{i}}\left(\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})^{\top}\hat{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}

(
\left\|\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right\|_{\infty}\leq B
and
\left\|\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(s_{i})\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}
.)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}_{i}\sim U(\mathcal{A}_{i})}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{s\sim P_{h-1}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}_{i}\sim U(\mathcal{A}_{i})}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}+nB^{2}\xi^{(n)}

(Event
\mathcal{E}
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},s\sim P_{h-1}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}_{i}\sim U(\mathcal{A}_{i})}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}+B^{2}n\xi^{(n)}.

(Jensen)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}+B^{2}n\zeta^{(n)}.

(Definition of
\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}
)

Combing the above results together, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{i})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\Bigg[\min\Bigg\{\tilde{A}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\hat{w}^{(n)}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt,B\Bigg\}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\sqrt{n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}+B^{2}n\zeta^{(n)}},B\right\}\right],

which has finished the proof.
∎

Lemma D.3

(One-step back inequality for the true model)
.

Consider a set of functions
\{g_{h}\}^{H}_{h=1}
that satisfies
g_{h}\in\mathcal{S}[Z_{i}]\times\mathcal{A}_{i}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
, s.t.
\|g_{h}\|_{\infty}\leq B
. Then for any policy
\pi
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\sqrt{\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]},\quad h=1,\\
&\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h-1}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}},\quad h\geq 2,\end{aligned}\right.

where
\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(s,\bm{a}):=\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\phi^{\star}_{h-1,j}(s[Z_{j}],\bm{a}_{j})
, and
\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}=n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\gamma^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d^{|Z_{i}|}}
.

Proof.

For step
h=1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},1}}\left[g_{1}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1},\bm{a}_{i}\sim\pi_{1}(s)}\left[g_{1}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\max_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})}\frac{d_{1}(s)\pi_{1}(\bm{a}_{i}|s)}{\rho^{(n)}_{1}(s,\bm{a}_{i})}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},\bm{a}^{\prime}_{i})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s^{\prime}[Z_{i}],\bm{a}^{\prime}_{i})\right]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\max_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})}\frac{d_{1}(s)\pi_{1}(\bm{a}_{i}|s)}{d_{1}(s)u_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}(\bm{a}_{i})}\mathbb{E}_{(s^{\prime},\bm{a}_{i}^{\prime})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s^{\prime}[Z_{i}],\bm{a}^{\prime}_{i})\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]}.

For step
h=2,\ldots,H-1
, we observe the following one-step-back decomposition:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}_{i})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h-1},s\sim P^{\star}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}_{i}\sim\pi_{h}(s)}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h-1}}\left[\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\phi^{\star}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})\right)^{\top}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}w^{\star}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)\pi_{h}(\bm{a}_{i}|s)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h-1}}\left[\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\phi^{\star}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})\right)^{\top}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}w^{\star}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h-1}}\left[\left\|\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\phi^{\star}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\cdot\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}w^{\star}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h-1},i}}.

Then,

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}w^{\star}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h-1,i}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\int_{\mathcal{S}}\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}w^{\star}_{h-1,j}(s_{j})\right)^{\top}\left(\bigotimes_{j\in Z_{i}}\phi^{\star}_{h-1,j}(\tilde{s}[Z_{j}],\tilde{\bm{a}}_{j})\right)g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\mathrm{d}s[Z_{i}]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}

(Use the assumption
\left\|\sum_{\bm{a}_{i}\in\mathcal{A}_{i}}\frac{1}{|\mathcal{A}_{i}|}g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right\|_{\infty}\leq B
and
\left\|w^{\star}_{h-1,i}(s_{i})\right\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{d}
.)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1}}\left[\left(\mathbb{E}_{s\sim P^{\star}_{h-1}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}_{i}\sim U(\mathcal{A}_{i})}\left[g_{h}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]\right)^{2}\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1},s\sim P_{h-1}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}}),\bm{a}_{i}\sim U(\mathcal{A}_{i})}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L}

(Jensen)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a}_{i})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L},

(Definition of
\rho^{(n)}_{h}
)

which has finished the proof.
∎

Lemma D.4

(One-step back inequality for the true model)
.

Consider a set of functions
\{g_{h}\}^{H}_{h=1}
that satisfies
g_{h}\in\mathcal{S}[\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}]\times\mathcal{A}[Z_{i}]\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
, s.t.
\|g_{h}\|_{\infty}\leq B
. Then for any policy
\pi
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[g_{h}(s[\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}],\bm{a}[Z_{i}])\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\sqrt{\tilde{A}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[g_{1}^{2}(s[\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}],\bm{a}[Z_{i}])\right]},\quad h=1,\\
&\tilde{A}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi}_{P^{\star},h-1}}\left[\left\|\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h-1},\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[g_{h}^{2}(s[\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}],\bm{a}[Z_{i}])\right]+B^{2}\lambda d^{L^{2}}},\quad h\geq 2,\end{aligned}\right.

where
\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(s,\bm{a}):=\bigotimes_{k\in\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}}\phi^{\star}_{h-1,j}(s[Z_{k}],\bm{a}_{k})
, and
\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}=n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\gamma^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d^{|\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}|}}
.

Proof.

This Lemma can be proved using similar steps as those in the proof of Lemma
D.3
, noting that in this case the dimension of
\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}
is at most
L^{2}
.
∎

Lemma D.5

(Optimism for NE and CCE)
.

Consider an episode
n\in[N]
and set
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\tilde{A}\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda}\right)
. When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds and the policy
\pi^{(n)}
is computed by solving NE or CCE, we have

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}(s)-v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}(s)\geq-HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}},\quad\forall n\in[N],i\in[M].

Proof.

Denote
\tilde{\mu}_{h,i}^{(n)}(\cdot|s):=\argmax_{\mu}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mu,\pi_{h,-i}^{(n)}}Q^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{h,i}\right)(s)
and let
\tilde{\pi}_{h}^{(n)}=\tilde{\mu}^{(n)}_{h,i}\times\pi_{h,-i}^{(n)}
. Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}
and
f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})-P^{\star}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right\|_{1}
. Then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],i\in[M]

\displaystyle\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\bar{\phi}_{h,i}\in\bar{\Phi}_{h,i},i\in[M].

A direct conclusion of the event
\mathcal{E}
is we can find an absolute constant
c
, such that

\displaystyle\beta_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}},H\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}^{-1}},H\right\},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H].

Next, similar to the proof in Lemma
B.5
, we may prove

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{1,i}(s)\right]\geq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right].

For the second term, note that we have the relation
\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{M}\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}
. By Lemma
D.2
, we have for
h=1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[\min\left\{f_{1,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}\right]\leq\sqrt{A\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho_{1}^{(n)}}\left[\left(f_{1,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]}\leq\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}.

And
\forall h\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\min\left\{f_{h,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\sqrt{n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f_{h,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]+d^{L}\lambda+n\zeta^{(n)}},1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\tilde{A}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda},1\right\}\right]

Note that we here use
\min\{f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\}\leq 1,\ \mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
and
\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
. Then according to our choice of
\alpha^{(n)}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\min\left\{f_{h,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\min\left\{\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}},1\right\}\right].

Combining all things together,

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\sum_{j=1}^{M}\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,j}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma_{\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,j}}^{-1}},H\right\}\right]-HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}},

which proves the inequality.
∎

Lemma D.6

(Optimism for CE)
.

Consider an episode
n\in[N]
and set
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\tilde{A}\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda}\right)
. When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds, we have

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}(s)\geq-HM\sqrt{A\zeta^{(n)}},\quad\forall n\in[N],i\in[M].

Proof.

Denote
\tilde{\omega}_{h,i}^{(n)}=\argmax_{\omega_{h}\in\Omega_{h,i}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\omega_{h}\circ\pi_{h}^{(n)}}\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}Q_{h,i}^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}\right)(s)
and let
\tilde{\pi}_{h}^{(n)}=\tilde{\omega}_{h,i}\circ\pi^{(n)}_{h}
. Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}
and
f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})-P^{\star}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right\|_{1}
. Then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],i\in[M]

\displaystyle\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\bar{\phi}_{h,i}\in\bar{\Phi}_{h,i},i\in[M].

A direct conclusion of the event
\mathcal{E}
is we can find an absolute constant
c
, such that

\displaystyle\beta_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}},H\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle c\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}^{-1}},H\right\},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H].

Next, similar to the proof in Lemma
B.6
, we may prove

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]\geq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right].

Note that we can use exactly the same steps in the proof of Lemma
D.5
to bound the second term, and we get for
h=1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[\min\left\{f_{1,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}\right]\leq\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}.

And
\forall h\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\min\left\{f_{h,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}\right]\leq\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\right].

Combining all things together,

\displaystyle\overline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\overline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}V^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]-H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}}\left[\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})-\sum_{j=1}^{M}\min\left(c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,j}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma_{\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,j}}^{-1}},H\right)\right]-HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}},

which proves the inequality.
∎

Lemma D.7

(pessimism)
.

Consider an episode
n\in[N]
and set
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\tilde{A}\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda}\right)
. When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds, we have

\displaystyle\underline{v}_{i}^{(n)}(s)-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}(s)\leq HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}},\quad\forall n\in[N],i\in[M].

Proof.

Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}
and
f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})-P^{\star}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right\|_{1}
. Then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],i\in[M]

\displaystyle\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\bar{\phi}_{h,i}\in\bar{\Phi}_{h,i},i\in[M].

A direct conclusion of the event
\mathcal{E}
is we can find an absolute constant
c
, such that

\displaystyle\beta_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\min\left\{\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\left(\Sigma_{h,\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}^{(n)}\right)^{-1}},H\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\min\left\{c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}^{-1}},H\right\},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H].

Next, similar to the proof in Lemma
B.7
, we may prove

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\underline{V}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{h,i}(s)\right]\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}=h}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h^{\prime}}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h^{\prime}}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+H\min\left\{f^{(n)}_{h^{\prime}}(s,\bm{a}),1\right\}\right],\quad\forall h\in[H].

(23)

and we get for
h=1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},1}}\left[\min\left\{f_{1,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}\right]\leq\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}.

And
\forall h\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}}\left[\min\left\{f_{h,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i}),1\right\}\right]\leq\frac{c\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\tilde{\pi}^{(n)}}_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h-1}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h-1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h-1,i}}^{-1}}\right].

Finally, we get

\displaystyle\underline{v}_{i}^{(n)}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d_{1}}\left[\underline{V}_{1,i}^{(n)}(s)-V^{\pi^{(n)}}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{\hat{P}^{(n)},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[-\hat{\beta}_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})+\sum_{j=1}^{M}\min\left(c\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,j}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma_{\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,j}}^{-1}},H\right)\right]+HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}},

which has finished the proof.
∎

Lemma D.8
.

When the event
\mathcal{E}
holds and
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\tilde{A}\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda}\right)
satisfies
\alpha^{(1)}\leq\alpha^{(2)}\leq\ldots\leq\alpha^{(N)}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim H^{2}Md^{L^{2}}A^{L}\sqrt{N\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}.

Proof.

Let
f^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})-P^{\star}_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})\right\|_{1}
and
f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})=\left\|\hat{P}^{(n)}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})-P^{\star}_{h,i}(\cdot|s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right\|_{1}
. Then according to the event
\mathcal{E}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\tilde{\rho}^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)},\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],i\in[M]

\displaystyle\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}\right)^{-1}}=\Theta\left(\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}_{h,i}}}\right),\quad\forall n\in[N],h\in[H],\bar{\phi}_{h,i}\in\bar{\Phi}_{h,i},i\in[M].

By definition, we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n)}=\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}\right\}+2HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}.

With similar steps as those in the proof of Lemma
B.8
(note that
\overline{V}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s)
is upper bounded by
2H^{2}M
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)\right]\leq 2\underbrace{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]}_{(a)}+2H^{2}M\underbrace{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d_{P^{\star},h}^{\pi^{(n)}}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]}_{(b)}.

(24)

First, we calculate the first term (a) in Inequality equation
24
. Following Lemma
D.4
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\min\left(\alpha^{(n)}\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}},H\right)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\tilde{A}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\cdot\sqrt{{n\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}}\right]+H^{2}d^{L^{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sqrt{\tilde{A}^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{1}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{1},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{1,i}}}\right]}.

Note that we use the fact that
B=H
when applying Lemma
D.3
. In addition, we have

\displaystyle n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right\|^{2}_{\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{(n)}_{h,i}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\textrm{Tr}\left(\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]\left(n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})^{\top}\right]+\lambda I_{d^{|Z_{i}|}}\right)^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d^{L}.

Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\hat{\beta}^{(n)}_{h}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\tilde{A}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\tilde{\Sigma}_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{d^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d^{L^{2}}\lambda}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{{d^{L}\tilde{A}^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}/n}.

Second, we calculate the term (b) in inequality equation
24
. Following Lemma
D.3
and noting
f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})
is upper-bounded by
2
(i.e.,
B=2
in Lemma
D.3
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[f_{h}^{(n)}(s,\bm{a})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[f_{h,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{h,i}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]+d^{L}\lambda}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}\left[\left(f^{(n)}_{1}(s[Z_{j}],\bm{a}_{j})\right)^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\tilde{A}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda}+\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}},

where in the second inequality, we use
\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho_{h}^{(n)}}\left[\left(f_{h,i}^{(n)}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\right)^{2}\right]\leq\zeta^{(n)}
, and in the last line, recall
\tilde{A}\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda}\lesssim\alpha^{(n)}/H
. Then, by combining the above calculation of the term (a) and term (b) in inequality equation
24
, we have:

\displaystyle\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},1}}\left[\overline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)-\underline{V}^{(n)}_{1,i}(s)\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\tilde{A}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{d^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d^{L^{2}}\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{d^{L}\tilde{A}^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{2}M\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}\right).

Taking maximum over
i
on both sides and use the definition of
\Delta^{(n)}
, we get

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n)}=

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}\right\}+2HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\tilde{A}^{L}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\tilde{\Sigma}_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\sqrt{d^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}+H^{2}d^{L^{2}}\lambda}+\sqrt{\frac{d^{L}\tilde{A}^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{2}M\sum_{i=1}^{M}\sum_{h=1}^{H-1}\left(\frac{\alpha^{(n)}}{H}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}^{\star}_{h,i}}^{-1}}\right]+\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}\right).

Hereafter, we take the dominating term out. Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{N\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}}\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right]}

(CS inequality)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{N\left(\log\det\left(\lambda I_{d^{|\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}|}}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\tilde{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})^{\top}\right]\right)-\log\det(\lambda I_{d^{|\cup_{j\in Z_{i}}Z_{j}|}})\right)}

(Lemma
E.2
)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{d^{L^{2}}N\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}.

(Potential function bound, Lemma
E.3
noting
\|\phi_{h,i}^{\star}(s[Z_{i}],\bm{a}_{i})\|_{2}\leq 1
for any
(s,\bm{a})
.)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\sim d^{\pi^{(n)}}_{P^{\star},h}}\left[\left\|\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}(\tilde{s},\tilde{\bm{a}})\right\|_{\Sigma_{n,\gamma^{(n)}_{h},\bar{\phi}_{h,i}^{\star}}^{-1}}\right]\leq\sqrt{d^{L}N\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}.

Finally,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim

\displaystyle HM\left(\sqrt{d^{L^{2}}N\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\tilde{A}^{L}\sqrt{{d^{L}\left(\alpha^{(N)}\right)^{2}}+H^{2}d^{L^{2}}\lambda}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sqrt{\frac{d^{L}\tilde{A}^{L}\left(\alpha^{(n)}\right)^{2}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle+H^{3}M^{2}\left(\frac{1}{H}\sqrt{d^{L}N\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}+\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle H^{2}M^{2}d^{L^{2}}\tilde{A}^{L}\sqrt{N\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}.

(Some algebra. We take the dominating term out. Note that
\alpha^{(n)}
is increasing in
n
)

This concludes the proof.
∎

D.3
Proof of the Main Theorems

Lemma D.9
.

For the model-based algorithm, when we pick
\lambda=\Theta\left(Ld^{L}\log\frac{NHM|\Phi|}{\delta}\right)
,
\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\tilde{A}\sqrt{n\zeta^{(n)}+d^{L}\lambda}\right)
and
\zeta^{(n)}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{n}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HNM}{\delta}\right)
, with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim H^{3}M^{2}d^{(L+1)^{2}}\tilde{A}^{\frac{L+1}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HNM}{\delta}.

Proof.

The result of Lemma
D.1
implies with our choice of
\lambda
and
\zeta^{(n)}
, the event
\mathcal{E}
holds with probability at least
1-\delta
. In this case, we have

\displaystyle\alpha^{(n)}=\Theta\left(H\tilde{A}\sqrt{\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HNM}{\delta}+Ld^{2L}\log\frac{NHM|\Phi|}{\delta}}\right),

(25)

which is a constant unrelated with
n
. Therefore, using the result of Lemma
D.8
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim H^{2}d^{L^{2}}\tilde{A}^{L}M^{2}\sqrt{N\log\left(1+\frac{N}{d\lambda}\right)}\alpha^{(N)}\lesssim H^{3}M^{2}d^{(L+1)^{2}}\tilde{A}^{L+1}L^{\frac{1}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HNM}{\delta},

which has finished the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
4.1

Proof.

For any fixed episode
n
and agent
i
, by Lemma
D.5
, Lemma
D.6
and Lemma
D.7
, we have

\displaystyle v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right)\leq\overline{v}^{(n)}_{i}-\underline{v}^{(n)}_{i}+2HM\sqrt{\tilde{A}\zeta^{(n)}}\leq\Delta^{(n)}.

Taking maximum over
i
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n)}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n)}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n)}}_{i}\right\}\right)\leq\Delta^{(n)}.

(26)

From Lemma
B.8
, with probability
1-\delta
, we can ensure

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\lesssim H^{3}M^{2}d^{(L+1)^{2}}\tilde{A}^{L+1}L^{\frac{1}{2}}N^{\frac{1}{2}}\log\frac{|\mathcal{M}|HNM}{\delta}.

Therefore, according to Lemma
E.4
, when we pick
N
to be

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{L^{5}M^{4}H^{6}d^{2(L+1)^{2}}\tilde{A}^{2(L+1)}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{HdALM|\mathcal{M}|}{\delta\varepsilon}\right)\right),

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\leq\varepsilon.

On the other hand, from equation
26
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\hat{\pi}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\hat{\pi}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\hat{\pi}}_{i}-v^{\hat{\pi}}_{i}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{v^{\dagger,\pi^{(n^{\star})}_{-i}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}\right\}\left(\textrm{or }\max_{i\in[M]}\left\{\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{i}}v^{\omega\circ\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}-v^{\pi^{(n^{\star})}}_{i}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Delta^{(n^{\star})}=\min_{n\in[N]}\Delta^{(n)}\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\Delta^{(n)}\leq\varepsilon,

which has finished the proof, noting our assumption that
L=O(1)
.
∎

Appendix E
Auxiliary Lemmas

Lemma E.1

(Concentration of the bonus term (
Zanette et al. 2021
, Lemma 39))
.

Set
\lambda^{(n)}\geq\Theta(d\log(nH|\Phi|/\delta))
for any
n
. Define

\displaystyle\Sigma_{n,\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi}=n\mathbb{E}_{(s,\bm{a})\sim\rho^{(n)}_{h}}[\phi(s,\bm{a})\phi^{\top}(s,\bm{a})]+\lambda^{(n)}I_{d},\quad\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi(s_{h}^{(i)},\bm{a}_{h}^{(i)})\phi^{\top}(s_{h}^{(i)},\bm{a}_{h}^{(i)})+\lambda^{(n)}I_{d}.

With probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\forall n\in\mathbb{N}^{+},\forall h\in[H],\forall\phi\in\Phi,\quad c_{1}\|\phi(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi}}\leq\|\phi(s,\bm{a})\|_{\left(\hat{\Sigma}^{(n)}_{h,\phi}\right)^{-1}}\leq c_{2}\|\phi(s,\bm{a})\|_{\Sigma^{-1}_{\rho^{(n)}_{h},\phi}}.

Lemma E.2

(
Agarwal et al. 2020a
, Lemma G.2)
.

Consider the following process. For
n=1,\ldots,N
,
M_{n}=M_{n-1}+G_{n}
with
M_{0}=\lambda_{0}I
and
G_{n}
being a positive semidefinite matrix with eigenvalues upper bounded by
1
. We have

\displaystyle 2\log\det(M_{N})-2\log\det(\lambda_{0}I)\geq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\mathrm{Tr}(G_{n}M^{-1}_{n-1}).

Lemma E.3

(Potential function lemma)
.

Suppose
\mathrm{Tr}(G_{n})\leq B^{2}
.

\displaystyle 2\log\det(M_{N})-2\log\det(\lambda_{0}I)\leq d\log\left(1+\frac{NB^{2}}{d\lambda_{0}}\right)

Proof.

Let
\sigma_{1},\cdots,\sigma_{d}
be the set of singular values of
M_{N}
recalling
M_{N}
is a positive semidefinite matrix. Then, by the AM-GM inequality,

\displaystyle\log\det(M_{N})/\det(\lambda_{0}I)=\log\prod_{i=1}^{d}(\sigma_{i}/\lambda_{0})\leq\log d\left(\frac{1}{d}\sum_{i=1}^{d}(\sigma_{i}/\lambda_{0}))\right)

Since we have
\sum_{i}\sigma_{i}=\mathrm{Tr}(M_{N})\leq d\lambda_{0}+NB^{2}
, the statement is concluded.
∎

Lemma E.4
.

For parameters
A,B,\varepsilon
such that
\frac{A^{2}B}{\varepsilon^{2}}
is larger than some absolute constant, when we pick
N=\frac{A^{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{A^{4}B^{2}}{\varepsilon^{4}}=O\left(\frac{A^{2}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{AB}{\varepsilon}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{A}{\sqrt{N}}\log(BN)\leq\varepsilon.

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\frac{A}{\sqrt{N}}\log(BN)=\varepsilon\frac{\log\left(\frac{A^{2}B}{\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{A^{4}B^{2}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\right)}{\log\frac{A^{4}B^{2}}{\varepsilon^{4}}}

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{A^{2}B}{\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{2}\frac{A^{4}B^{2}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\leq\frac{A^{4}B^{2}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\Leftrightarrow\log^{2}\frac{A^{4}B^{2}}{\varepsilon^{4}}\leq\frac{A^{2}B}{\varepsilon^{2}}

where the right hand side is always true whenever
